- An interrupted release can bump `CFBundleVersion` without archiving/uploading; check App Store Connect's latest build before bumping again
- Before upload, grep the release binary for new endpoint markers to confirm they shipped
- `.github/workflows/announce.yml` posts commits pushed to main to the stupid social room (`1793555674934`) on `wss://server.apron.chat/`, as the bot `stupid-bot` (`APRON_BOT_TOKEN`, a secret of the `announce` environment), each tagged `ext["stupid-social-announce"] = "<repo>@<push head>"`. A pushed `testflight/<build>` tag posts nothing: it reacts ✈️ to the announcements whose head is the tag's commit or an ancestor and not the previous testflight tag's, read from the last 400 history records. `apron-chat/apron-pr-bot` only announces merged pull requests, so `.github/scripts/apron-announce.mjs` is a dependency-free copy of its client and `deployed` logic. The bot must have joined the room (`room_join`); stable request ids keep reruns from posting or reacting twice. A push whose head lands after the bump commit isn't in that build, so it gets the reaction with the next one

## Probes, scripts & debug tooling

### General
- Probes are dependency-free Python (except `xchat-client.py`); summaries by default with credential values redacted; `--output` writes under `logs/`. Captures, tokens and caches under `logs/` must not be committed
- `scripts/x-web-client.py`: `search-users` (`--simulator --summary`), follow/unfollow/bell commands; `--env-local` reads credentials from `DIAGNOSE_X_CREDS`
- `scripts/xchat-client.py` validates X Chat end to end: Juicebox recovery, inbox paging, decryption, signatures, franking, send/react/read, `listen`

### Instagram
- `scripts/instagram-web-client.py`: `--simulator` reads fallback credentials; iPhone Safari UA by default; discovers doc IDs from `*_instagramRelayOperation` definitions with no hardcoded fallbacks; `homepage-tray` checks the homepage viewer and story tray the way the app parses them. Doc ID/CSRF cache `logs/instagram-web-client-cache.json` (1 h TTL; `--cache-file`, `--cache-ttl-seconds`, `--no-cache`)
- Commands: `bootstrap`, `refresh`, `docids`, `session-check`, `activity`, `news-inbox`, `stories-tray`, `story-page`, `current-user`, `profile-username`, `mention-search`, `follow`/`unfollow`, `story-seen` (V3 mutation, exits non-zero on null root), `force-story-seen`, `like-story`/`unlike-story`, `like-media`/`unlike-media`, `upload-story-image [--close-friends]`, `delete-story` (mobile REST, bare or owner-suffixed IDs, `--video`, `--story-username` loads the story page first), raw `graphql`
- Direct: `direct-inbox [--pages N]`, `direct-thread <thread_fbid> [--cursor C]`, `direct-seen <thread_fbid> <message_id>`, `direct-send-text <thread_fbid> <text> [--reply-to mid]`, `direct-react`
- `current-user` resolves the viewer from the Direct inbox only; the web `stories-tray`/current-user endpoints are broken (observed 2026-10-26)
- `parse_web_state` records `page_user_id` and `actor_id`; `graphql_post` sends `__user` from the page plus `av`. Retries once after fresh bootstrap on 400/401/403; `--save-credentials` exports rotated cookies
- Fails loudly: "Instagram session expired" when any response (incl. redirects) expires `sessionid`; "Instagram requires login" on checkpoint/challenge/`login_required` or `not-logged-in` bodies
- Android experiment: `scripts/android-instagram-mitm.sh` (mitmproxy on `8888` since 8080 is taken on macOS; emulator proxy `10.0.2.2:8888`; system CA needs `-writable-system`, `adb root`, `adb remount`; `--ignore-hosts` for pinned Meta domains; clear with `adb -s emulator-5556 shell settings put global http_proxy :0`). Frida scripts `scripts/instagram-frida-*.js` redact cookies/tokens

### Spotify
- `scripts/spotify-web-client.py` reads `tech.stupid.StupidSocial.credentials.spotify.localFallback`; ports the TOTP algorithm (`totpVer=61`, self-test `031750` at timestamp `1777993436`); full JSON by default (`--summary` for compact)
- Auth/identity: `validate-cookie-login` (token renewal and friend activity from selected cookies, redacted), `client-token` (mint Pathfinder client-token), `username`/`resolve-username` (via Pathfinder), `current-username` (`/v1/me`, rate-limited)
- Also probes listening activity (and the legacy buddylist), profile attributes, user/follow, audio analysis, library save/add/remove Pathfinder calls, custom spclient GETs
- Messages: `chats [--limit N]`, `chat <id|spotify:chat:URI> [--page-size N] [--page-token T]`, `chat-send`, `chat-seen`, `share-metadata <uri>`, `track-metadata`, `track-preview`; dependency-free protobuf codec with a `SPEECHLESS_SCHEMA` subset; `speechless_post` retries after token refresh on 401/403

### GitHub & Bluesky
- `scripts/github-web-client.py`: reads a full Cookie header but keeps only session cookies; `notifications` (`extract_starred_your_repo` incl. rollup sibling propagation, `repo_metadata_from_html`, `follow_user_metadata_from_html`, `created` verb) at parity with the Swift parser; `validate` (`NoRedirectHandler`, `--simulator`) reports `status` `valid`/`expired`/`error` with `http_status`, `location`, `feed_item_count`, replays `additionalCookies` and reports `refreshed_user_session`
- Simulator credentials (`scripts/sim_keychain.py`, used by the GitHub probe's `--simulator`, `--account-id` when several): the simulator build keeps credentials in the keychain under `group.tech.stupid.StupidSocial`, one item per account id, and writes the UserDefaults `…credentials.<account id>.localFallback` only when a keychain write fails, so on a working simulator there is no fallback to read. The module finds the network's account in `accounts.entries`, then reads the item with `scripts/sim-keychain-read.swift`, built for the simulator into `logs/` and ad-hoc signed with only the app group entitlement, through `xcrun simctl spawn booted`; without that entitlement `SecItemCopyMatching` returns -34018 (observed 2026-10-31). The X, Instagram, Spotify and Bluesky probes still read the pre-multi-account fallback keys
- `scripts/bluesky-web-client.py` reads `tech.stupid.StupidSocial.credentials.bluesky.localFallback` via `sim-prefs`, signs DPoP proofs, retries nonces; `notifications`, `post-thread`, `profile`. `scripts/bluesky-oauth-par-probe.py <handle> --client-id <url>` verifies PAR without completing login

### Apron
- `scripts/apron-web-client.py`: dependency-free WebSocket client; redacts `token` fields, keeps issued tokens in ignored `logs/apron-probe-token`. P-256, HKDF and AES-128-GCM are dependency-free, checked against FIPS-197 and RFC 8291 Appendix A
- Commands: `hello <url>`, `limits <url> --token-file ""` (docs/limits.md shapes: connection-status probe, guest permissions, pong, a `retry_after` from `room_list`), `rooms <url> [--members] [-v]`, `history` (prints `_elapsed_s`), `join`, `listen`, `seed`, `upload`, `react`, `typing`, `typing-rate --count --interval --stops`, `me --avatar`, `room-create` (`room_set`, `--private`, `--title`, `--add` via `room_join`), `dm --user` (finds or starts the DM like the app), `email-signup` (reads the code from the server log, approves with `--user-id`/`--name`, token to `--token-file`)
- `send` options: `--mention`, `--reply-to`, `--image-url --image-size` (`--image-size none` sends `og.image` without a size), `--link-url`/`--link-title`/`--link-description`/`--link-image` for link-preview embeds
- Push: `push-register`, `push-unregister`, `mute [--room R] [--off]`, `push-capture` (local endpoint that logs headers and decrypts bodies)
- Signing the probe in with the simulator's saved token failed ("Frontend origin is not configured for passkeys") (observed 2026-10-26)
- Local server: `scripts/apron-local-server.sh` builds `third-party/apron-server-go` and serves `0.0.0.0:8090`; state/log in `logs/apron-local/`, email codes to the log (`--email.sender log`), any origin allowed (native clients send none), push to the real relay. Memory-store alternative: `go run ./cmd/aprond --store memory --push.allow-insecure --addr 127.0.0.1:8090`
- Lighter alternative: Bun `third-party/apron/servers/js-minimal/apron-server.js` (images from `127.0.0.1:8770`; no `embed:upload`, no `reactions`, keeps 1,000 records; passes through finished upload embeds with `og.image`). `SIGSTOP` on the server tests cache-only rendering; `lsof` checks connection count

### Telegram
- `scripts/telegram-client.py`: stdlib-only MTProto (pure-Python AES-IGE, RSA_PAD, DH, Pollard–Brent, schema-driven TL, SRP). Commands include `handshake`, `dialogs [--archived]`, `listen [--typing]`, `typing --peer [--cancel]`, `download`, `avatar`, `send-photo`, `send-voice --peer self --file F.ogg --duration S [--levels …]`, `forward --from-peer K --ids N… --to-peer K [--drop-author]`, `forwards [--dialogs N]` (which `messageFwdHeader` fields received forwards carry; no names or text), `react`, `peer-status --peer user:<id>`, `members --peer …`, `stickers [--set NAME]`, `stickers-for --emoji 😂`, `sticker-corpus [--set NAME…] [--animated-emoji] [--format tgs|webm|webp|all] -o DIR`, `send-sticker --peer self [--document-id ID]`, `webpage-preview --url U [--download-dir D]` (a link preview's type, `has_large_media` and media as the app reads them, downloading each). Reads `TELEGRAM_USER_PASSWORD`, `TELEGRAM_APP_ID`, `TELEGRAM_APP_API_HASH` from `.env.local`. Probe and app are separate Telegram authorizations

### Stickers
- These scripts are in the StupidChatKit repository, with the renderers; the corpus comes from here (`scripts/telegram-client.py sticker-corpus`)
- `scripts/lottie-census.py DIR [--cover]`: which Lottie features a TGS corpus uses, and a small set of stickers covering all of them
- `scripts/lottie-compare.py FILES [--frames N] [--size PX] [--threshold %]`: builds Telegram's rlottie fork (`third-party/rlottie-telegram`, cmake, its NEON assembly off) with `scripts/lottie/reference.cpp`, and the app's renderer as a Mac tool (`scripts/lottie/render.swift`, also `--time FILE PX`), renders evenly spaced frames with both and compares them with ImageMagick; `logs/lottie-compare/report.html` shows the worst first
- `scripts/webm-compare.sh FILES`: the app's WebM decoder (`scripts/stickers/webm.swift`, also `--time`) against `ffmpeg -c:v libvpx-vp9` on the first, middle and last frames
- Corpora go in `logs/sticker-corpus*/` (other people's artwork; never committed). Tests use synthetic fixtures (`StickerTests`)

### Simulator debugging
- Layout/scroll jumps: record with `simctl io recordVideo` and check frame by frame. Open jumps (2026-11-22) were reproduced on the local Apron server: post as another user while the app is in the background, leave the messages in the App Group `chat-inbox/` as the extension would (JSON `InboxEntry`), `kill -STOP` the server while foregrounding and opening the chat, `kill -CONT` 1.5 s later, then diff consecutive frames (`magick compare -metric AE`) to find movement after the push
- After `simctl privacy grant`, the pasteboard service (`pasted`) can hang so `UITextView.becomeFirstResponder` blocks in `_supportsImagePaste` → `PBServerConnection` and the app freezes; killing `pasted` doesn't help, rebooting the simulator does
